{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"resounds","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"resounding","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"resounded","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"resounded","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To make (sounds), or to speak (words), loudly or reverberatingly."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["Of a place: to cause (a sound) to reverberate; to echo."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To praise or spread the fame of (someone or something) with the voice or the sound of musical instruments; to celebrate, to extol; also, to declare (someone) to be a certain thing."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To repeat (another's words, opinions, etc.)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"echo"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The street resounded with the noise of the children’s game.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of a place: to reverberate with sound or noise."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["Of a sound, a voice, etc.: to reverberate; to ring."],"synonyms":[{"word":"echo"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The sound of the brass band resounded through the town.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Especially of a musical instrument: to make a (deep or reverberating) sound; also, to make sounds continuously."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["Of an event: to have a major effect in a certain place or time."],"tags":["figuratively","intransitive"]},{"glosses":["Of a person, their reputation, etc.: to be much lauded or mentioned."],"tags":["figuratively","intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹɪˈzaʊnd/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɹəˈzaʊnd/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ɹi-/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":410422,"upstream_offset":1958964286,"upstream_record_sha256":"3c88e0e77a3c1c122a2e6b279f580499f3c941fde20d0841f93a0cb727bda4d3","word":"resound"}
